```
- dataviz: Use this skill whenever you are about to create ANY chart, graph, plot, dashboard, or data visualization, in ANY output medium — an HTML or React artifact, inline SVG, plotting code in any library (matplotlib, plotly, d3, Recharts, …), an image/PNG you will render and upload, or a chart shared into Slack. Read it BEFORE writing the first line of chart code, choosing chart colors, building a stat tile / meter / KPI row, or laying out a dashboard. Produces visualizations that read as one system — elegant, accessible, consistent in light and dark — using a brand-neutral placeholder palette you swap for your own. Teaches a design-system-agnostic method: a form heuristic, a color formula with a runnable validator, mark specs, and interaction rules. A validated default palette is documented in `references/palette.md` — swap that file's values for your brand's. Triggers on: "chart", "graph", "plot", "data viz", "visualization", "dashboard", "analytics", "visualize data", "categorical colors", "sequential / diverging palette", "stat tile", "sparkline", "heatmap", "legend", "axis", "tooltip", "chart colors", "color by series".
- update-config: Use this skill to configure the Claude Code harness via settings.json. Automated behaviors ("from now on when X", "each time X", "whenever X", "before/after X") require hooks configured in settings.json - the harness executes these, not Claude, so memory/preferences cannot fulfill them. Also use for: permissions ("allow X", "add permission", "move permission to"), env vars ("set X=Y"), hook troubleshooting, or any changes to settings.json/settings.local.json files. Examples: "allow npm commands", "add bq permission to global settings", "move permission to user settings", "set DEBUG=true", "when claude stops show X". For simple settings like theme/model, suggest the /config command.
- keybindings-help: Use when the user wants to customize keyboard shortcuts, rebind keys, add chord bindings, or modify ~/.claude/keybindings.json. Examples: "rebind ctrl+s", "add a chord shortcut", "change the submit key", "customize keybindings".
- simplify: Review the changed code for reuse, simplification, efficiency, and altitude cleanups, then apply the fixes. Quality only — it does not hunt for bugs; use /code-review for that.
- fewer-permission-prompts: Scan your transcripts for common read-only Bash and MCP tool calls, then add a prioritized allowlist to project .claude/settings.json to reduce permission prompts.
- loop: Run a prompt or slash command on a recurring interval (e.g. /loop 5m /foo, defaults to 10m) - When the user wants to set up a recurring task, poll for status, or run something repeatedly on an interval (e.g. "check the deploy every 5 minutes", "keep running /babysit-prs"). Do NOT invoke for one-off tasks.
- claude-api: Reference for the Claude API / Anthropic SDK — model ids, pricing, params, streaming, tool use, MCP, agents, caching, token counting, model migration.
TRIGGER — read BEFORE opening the target file; don't skip because it "looks like a one-liner" — whenever: the prompt names Claude/Anthropic in any form (Claude, Anthropic, Fable, Opus, Sonnet, Haiku, `anthropic`, `@anthropic-ai`, `claude-*`, `us.anthropic.*`, `[1m]`); the user asks about an LLM (pricing/model choice/limits/caching) — never answer from memory; OR the task is LLM-shaped with provider unstated (agent/MCP/tool-definition/multi-agent/RAG/LLM-judge/computer-use; generate/summarize/extract/classify/rewrite/converse over NL; debugging refusals/cutoffs/streaming/tool-calls/tokens).
SKIP only when another provider is being worked on (overrides all triggers): OpenAI/GPT/Gemini/Llama/Mistral/Cohere/Ollama named in the query; OR `grep -rE 'openai|langchain_openai|google.generativeai|genai|mistralai|cohere|ollama'` over the project hits (run this grep FIRST if no provider named — don't Read the file).
- run: Launch and drive this project's app to see a change working. Use when asked to run, start, or screenshot the app, or to confirm a change works in the real app (not just tests). First looks for a project skill that already covers launching the app; otherwise falls back to built-in patterns per project type (CLI, server, TUI, Electron, browser-driven, library).
- init: Initialize a new CLAUDE.md file with codebase documentation
- review: Review a GitHub pull request; for your working diff use /code-review
- security-review: Complete a security review of the pending changes on the current branch
```

```
//   历史教训两连：

//   ② 改 xsb cue 配对后仍错位——vgmstream -s 是 1 基而 xsb 的 wave 索引是 0 基，
//     所有 id≥5 的文件整体前移一格（Music_8=腐化 装的是 Music_7=丛林，玩家在腐化之地
//     听到丛林 BGM）；且 xsb 前三条 cue 配对本身也是错的（M1/M3 对调）。

function streamNameMap() {

  return map;
}
```

```
57	
58	export class Sfx {
59	  private ctx: AudioContext | null = null;
60	  private buffers = new Map<string, AudioBuffer>();
61	  /** 在途解码中的文件（防重复 fetch） */
62	  private pending = new Set<string>();
63	  /** 取回失败/不存在的文件（负缓存，防 404 每次重试） */
64	  private failed = new Set<string>();
65	  muted = false;
66	  /** 主音量 0..1（M6 设置页） */
67	  master = 1;
68	
69	  /** 按需取缓冲：未命中时后台 fetch+decode，同步返回已就位缓冲或 null */
70	  private ensureBuffer(file: string): AudioBuffer | null {
71	    const buf = this.buffers.get(file);
72	    if (buf) return buf;
73	    if (this.pending.has(file) || this.failed.has(file)) return null;
74	    const ctx = this.ensure(true);
75	    if (!ctx) return null;
76	    this.pending.add(file);
77	    fetch(`sounds/${file}.wav`)
78	      .then((r) => (r.ok ? r.arrayBuffer() : null))
79	      .then((ab) => (ab ? ctx.decodeAudioData(ab) : null))
80	      .then((decoded) => {
81	        if (decoded) this.buffers.set(file, decoded);
82	        else this.failed.add(file);
83	      })
84	      .catch(() => { this.failed.add(file); })
85	      .finally(() => { this.pending.delete(file); });
86	    return null;
87	  }
88	
89	  /** 定向预热（不阻塞）：把指定 wav 后台取齐，播放时即命中 */
90	  preloadFiles(files: Iterable<string>) {
91	    for (const f of files) this.ensureBuffer(f);
92	  }
93	
94	  /** 按逻辑名预热（WAV_MAP 展开到具体文件） */
95	  preloadNames(names: SfxName[]) {
96	    for (const n of names) for (const f of WAV_MAP[n] ?? []) this.ensureBuffer(f);
97	  }
98	
99	  // ---- 雨声层：原版 RainAmbience（Music_28.mp3，XACT Wave Bank 同源提取）循环 ----
100	  private rainGain: GainNode | null = null;
101	  private rainSrc: AudioBufferSourceNode | null = null;
102	  private rainLoaded = false;
103	  /** 设置雨声强度 0-1（每帧调用；0 时静音不拆节点——重启有冷启动开销） */
104	  setRain(vol: number) {
105	    const ctx = this.ensure(true);
106	    if (!ctx) return;
107	    if (!this.rainGain) {
108	      const g = ctx.createGain();
109	      g.gain.value = 0;
110	      g.connect(ctx.destination);
111	      this.rainGain = g;
112	      // 原版雨环境音轨（RainAmbience）懒加载循环；加载失败静默（雨声可选）
113	      fetch('audios/music/Music_28.mp3')
114	        .then((r) => (r.ok ? r.arrayBuffer() : null))
115	        .then((ab) => (ab ? ctx.decodeAudioData(ab) : null))
116	        .then((buf) => {
117	          if (!buf) return;
118	          const src = ctx.createBufferSource();
119	          src.buffer = buf;
120	          src.loop = true;
121	          src.connect(this.rainGain!);
122	          src.start();
123	          this.rainSrc = src;
124	          this.rainLoaded = true;
125	        })
126	        .catch(() => {});
127	    }
128	    const v = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, vol)) * this.master * 0.6;
129	    this.rainGain.gain.setTargetAtTime(v, ctx.currentTime, 0.4);
130	  }
131	
132	  /** 世界声距离衰减（LegacySoundPlayer.cs:160 SoundAttenuationDistance = 2500px） */
133	  static readonly ATTEN = 2500;
134	  /** 监听器（原版 Main.Camera.Center，相机中心非玩家）；Game 每帧更新。未进世界=(0,0) */
135	  private lx = 0; private ly = 0;
136	  setListener(x: number, y: number) { this.lx = x; this.ly = y; }
137	  /** 原版距离衰减（LegacySoundPlayer.cs:381-401）：dist ≥2500 → 不播；
138	   *  vol × (1 - dist/2500)。不传坐标（x=-1 语义）→ 全量（UI/玩家自身动作声） */
139	  private atten(vol: number, x?: number, y?: number): number {
140	    if (x == null || y == null) return vol;
141	    const d = Math.hypot(x - this.lx, y - this.ly);
142	    if (d >= Sfx.ATTEN) return 0;
143	    return vol * (1 - d / Sfx.ATTEN);
144	  }
145	
146	  private ensure(silent = false): AudioContext | null {
147	    if (this.muted && !silent) return null;
148	    if (!this.ctx) {
149	      const AC = window.AudioContext ?? (window as unknown as { webkitAudioContext: typeof AudioContext }).webkitAudioContext;
150	      if (!AC) return null;
151	      this.ctx = new AC();
152	    }
153	    if (this.ctx.state === 'suspended') this.ctx.resume().catch(() => {});
154	    return this.ctx;
155	  }
156	
157	  /** 播放原版 wav（随机变体 + 轻微音高扰动）；vol 0-1 */
158	  playWav(name: SfxName, vol = 1, x?: number, y?: number): boolean {
159	    const list = WAV_MAP[name];
160	    if (!list || !list.length) return false;
161	    return this.playWavFile(list[Math.floor(Math.random() * list.length)], vol, x, y);
162	  }
163	
164	  /** 播放任意 wav 文件列表（随机选 + 音高扰动）；供怪物专属声效用 */
165	  playFiles(files: string[], vol = 1, x?: number, y?: number): boolean {
166	    if (!files.length) return false;
167	    return this.playWavFile(files[Math.floor(Math.random() * files.length)], vol, x, y);
168	  }
169	
170	  /** 按文件名直播（缓冲未就位时触发后台加载，本播返回 false 走合成兜底） */
171	  playWavFile(file: string, vol = 1, x?: number, y?: number): boolean {
172	    const ctx = this.ensure();
173	    if (!ctx) return false;
174	    const v = this.atten(vol, x, y);
175	    if (v <= 0) return false;
176	    vol = v;
177	    const buf = this.ensureBuffer(file);
178	    if (!buf) return false;
179	    const src = ctx.createBufferSource();
180	    src.buffer = buf;
181	    src.playbackRate.value = 0.92 + Math.random() * 0.16;
182	    const g = ctx.createGain();
183	    g.gain.value = Math.min(1, vol * this.master);
184	    src.connect(g).connect(ctx.destination);
185	    src.start();
186	    return true;
187	  }
188	
189	  play(name: SfxName, vol = 1, x?: number, y?: number) {
190	    // 优先原版素材，失败回退合成（master 在 playWavFile/osc 各自生效一次）。
191	    // 带坐标时衰减（atten），≤0 直接不播——合成回退也不触发
192	    if (x != null && y != null && this.atten(vol, x, y) <= 0) return;
193	    if (this.playWav(name, vol, x, y)) return;
194	    const ctx = this.ensure();
195	    if (!ctx) return;
196	    const t = ctx.currentTime;
197	    const osc = (type: OscillatorType, f0: number, f1: number, dur: number, vol: number, delay = 0) => {
198	      const o = ctx.createOscillator();
199	      const g = ctx.createGain();
200	      o.type = type;
201	      o.frequency.setValueAtTime(f0, t + delay);
202	      o.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(Math.max(1, f1), t + delay + dur);
203	      g.gain.setValueAtTime(vol * this.master, t + delay);
204	      g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, t + delay + dur);
205	      o.connect(g).connect(ctx.destination);
206	      o.start(t + delay);
207	      o.stop(t + delay + dur);
208	    };
209	    switch (name) {
210	      case 'hit': osc('square', 320, 110, 0.09, 0.12); break;
211	      case 'chop': osc('triangle', 220, 80, 0.12, 0.16); break;
212	      case 'hurt': osc('sawtooth', 180, 60, 0.22, 0.14); break;
213	      case 'killed': osc('square', 260, 60, 0.25, 0.14); break;
214	      case 'pkilled': osc('sawtooth', 160, 40, 0.6, 0.18); break;
215	      case 'zombie': osc('sawtooth', 90, 55, 0.5, 0.1); break;
216	      case 'roar': osc('sawtooth', 70, 40, 0.9, 0.2); break;
217	      case 'pickup': osc('sine', 520, 990, 0.09, 0.1); osc('sine', 780, 1320, 0.08, 0.06, 0.05); break;
218	      case 'dig': osc('square', 140, 90, 0.05, 0.06); break;
219	      case 'tink': osc('sine', 900, 500, 0.06, 0.08); break;
220	      case 'place': osc('triangle', 260, 120, 0.08, 0.12); break;
221	      case 'drink': osc('sine', 300, 620, 0.06, 0.08); osc('sine', 420, 780, 0.06, 0.07, 0.07); osc('sine', 560, 940, 0.06, 0.06, 0.14); break;
222	      case 'splash': osc('sine', 700, 200, 0.18, 0.1); break;
223	      case 'explosion': {
224	        // 噪声爆发近似(原版 Item_14):一次性白噪 buffer + 低频 thump。
225	        // ★曾无此分支:按需加载首播(wav 还在 fetch)完全静音 = "炸弹没有音效"主因
226	        const dur = 0.5, n = Math.floor(ctx.sampleRate * dur);
227	        const buf = ctx.createBuffer(1, n, ctx.sampleRate);
228	        const d = buf.getChannelData(0);
229	        for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) d[i] = (Math.random() * 2 - 1) * Math.pow(1 - i / n, 2.2);
230	        const src = ctx.createBufferSource();
231	        src.buffer = buf;
232	        const lp = ctx.createBiquadFilter();
233	        lp.type = 'lowpass';
234	        lp.frequency.setValueAtTime(900, t);
235	        lp.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(120, t + dur);
236	        const g = ctx.createGain();
237	        g.gain.value = 0.5 * this.master;
238	        src.connect(lp).connect(g).connect(ctx.destination);
239	        src.start(t);
240	        osc('sine', 90, 35, 0.35, 0.35); // 低频冲击
241	        break;
242	      }
243	      case 'coin': osc('sine', 990, 1400, 0.07, 0.08); osc('sine', 1320, 1760, 0.09, 0.06, 0.05); break;
244	      case 'door_open': osc('triangle', 180, 320, 0.12, 0.1); break;
245	      case 'door_close': osc('triangle', 320, 160, 0.1, 0.1); break;
246	      case 'shatter': osc('square', 1400, 300, 0.15, 0.1); break;
247	      case 'drown': osc('sine', 400, 90, 0.4, 0.1); break;
248	    }
249	  }
250	}
251	

```

```
  /** GlowMask 叠画：与基础精灵同一变换（调用点在 drawEnemy 的 save 块内，

  private drawNpcGlow(e: Enemy, r: { img: CanvasImageSource; sx: number; sy: number; sw: number; sh: number }) {
```

```
      if (this.vy > 0 && ey < 0) this.vy -= accel;

  }
```

```
// 原版素材接入：按白名单从 terraria-assets 拷贝 PNG + 提取 TEdit 元数据 → public/sprites/vanilla.json
// 原版 Tiles 表无 .meta（不同于 Maples Unity 项目），按 textureGrid+frameGap 网格寻址：
//   帧 (col,row) 位于 (col*(gridX+gapX), row*(gridY+gapY))，帧尺寸 = textureGrid
// 用法：node scripts/vanilla-atlas.mjs
import { readFileSync, writeFileSync, copyFileSync, mkdirSync, existsSync, readdirSync, unlinkSync } from 'node:fs';
import { join, dirname } from 'node:path';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';

const here = dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
const ROOT = join(here, '..');
const ASSETS = join(ROOT, '..', 'terraria-assets', 'Images');
const TEDIT_DATA = join(ROOT, '..', 'Terraria-Map-Editor', 'src', 'TEdit.Terraria', 'Data');
const OUT_DIR = join(ROOT, 'public', 'sprites', 'vanilla');
const OUT_JSON = join(ROOT, 'public', 'sprites', 'vanilla.json');
const whitelist = JSON.parse(readFileSync(join(here, 'vanilla-whitelist.json'), 'utf8'));

/** 读 PNG IHDR（8 字节签名 + 4 长度 + 4 类型后即宽高，各 4 字节大端） */
function pngSize(file) {
  const b = readFileSync(file);
  return { w: b.readUInt32BE(16), h: b.readUInt32BE(20) };
}

const tiles = JSON.parse(readFileSync(join(TEDIT_DATA, 'tiles.json'), 'utf8'));
const items = JSON.parse(readFileSync(join(TEDIT_DATA, 'items.json'), 'utf8'));
const walls = JSON.parse(readFileSync(join(TEDIT_DATA, 'walls.json'), 'utf8'));
const tileById = new Map(tiles.map((t) => [t.id, t]));
const itemById = new Map(items.map((i) => [i.id, i]));
const wallById = new Map(walls.map((w) => [w.id, w]));

mkdirSync(OUT_DIR, { recursive: true });

// 杂项单图素材（呼吸气泡等，非表驱动）+ 树木专用贴图（TEdit WorldRenderXna 逻辑）
const MISC = [
  'Bubble.png',
  // 原版云朵（Cloud.cs 常用 0-3；9-13 雨云/18-21 风暴云随天气系统后补）
  'Cloud_0.png', 'Cloud_1.png', 'Cloud_2.png', 'Cloud_3.png',
  // 树冠/树枝（style 0 起步；TEdit GetTreeTops/GetTreeBranches）
  ...Array.from({ length: 32 }, (_, i) => `Tree_Tops_${i}.png`),
  ...Array.from({ length: 32 }, (_, i) => `Tree_Branches_${i}.png`),
  // 各生物群系树干（TEdit GetTree → Images/Tiles_5_N）
  ...Array.from({ length: 7 }, (_, i) => `Tiles_5_${i}.png`),
  // 仙人掌生物群系变体（腐化/神圣/猩红，与 Tiles_80 同 18px 网格）
  'Evil_Cactus.png', 'Good_Cactus.png', 'Crimson_Cactus.png',
  // 液体贴图（TEdit GetLiquid：0-13 群系水色 + 1 岩浆 11 蜂蜜 14 微光）
```

```
const hex = (c) => (c ? '#' + c.replace('#', '').slice(0, 6).toUpperCase() : '#808080');

const out = { tiles: {}, items: {}, walls: {}, npcs: {} };

// NPC 贴图表（纵向帧条）的帧高：原版帧数在二进制里，按已知贴图规格手配
const NPC_FRAME_H = {
  // 按透明分隔行实测（analyze-frames.mjs 探测）：帧高必须整除表高且对齐内容带
  46: 40, 74: 24, 299: 32, 300: 16, 355: 16, 356: 24,
  359: 20, 361: 22, 362: 40, 377: 12,
  442: 24, 443: 40, 444: 24, 445: 22, 446: 12, 447: 16,

  488: 50, // 训练假人：32x550，11 帧（0 站立 + 双向摇动 5+5）
  602: 36, // 海鸥：44x540，15 帧
  49: 32, // 洞穴蝙蝠：44x160，5 帧扑翼（带探测起始 ≈ 0/32/64/96/128）
};

let copied = 0, missing = [];

for (const id of whitelist.tiles) {
  const t = tileById.get(id);
  const src = join(ASSETS, `Tiles_${id}.png`);
  if (!t || !existsSync(src)) { missing.push(`Tiles_${id}`); continue; }
  copyFileSync(src, join(OUT_DIR, `Tiles_${id}.png`)); copied++;
  const grid = t.textureGrid ?? [16, 16];
  const gap = t.frameGap ?? [2, 2];
  const { w, h } = pngSize(src);
  out.tiles[id] = {
    name: t.name,
    key: t.key,
    sheet: `vanilla/Tiles_${id}.png`,
    solid: !!t.isSolid,
    blend: !!t.canBlend,
    framed: !!t.isFramed,
    light: !!t.isLight,
    color: hex(t.color),
    placement: t.placement ?? null,
    grid, // 帧像素尺寸（蜡烛类 [16,20]）
    stride: [grid[0] + gap[0], grid[1] + gap[1]], // 表内帧步长
    frameSize: t.frameSize ?? [[1, 1]], // 每个占格数（多格家具）
    isStone: !!t.isStone,   // 石族（同类互连判定）
    isGrass: !!t.isGrass,   // 草族（strictness 2 草规则）
    mergeWith: t.mergeWith ?? null, // 合并目标（blend framing）
    cols: Math.floor(w / (grid[0] + gap[0])),
    rows: Math.floor(h / (grid[1] + gap[1])),
  };
}

// 阶段5:物品全量——shelf-pack 打包成少数 Item_Atlas_k.png 图集(6059 张单体 → ~3 张)。
//  请求粒度优化:运行时 preloadIcons 只取图集,不再逐张 Item_N.png(此前 6000+ 网络请求)。
//  2048² 货架,按高度降序;2px gutter 防 UI 32×32 缩小采样串色;解码失败回退单体路径。
import { PNG } from 'pngjs';
const ATLAS_W = 2048, ATLAS_H = 2048, GUTTER = 2;
// 先 IHDR 读尺寸(不解码)→ 按高度降序排序,货架密度更高
const itemFiles = [];
for (const it of items) {
  const src = join(ASSETS, `Item_${it.id}.png`);
  if (!existsSync(src)) { missing.push(`Item_${it.id}`); continue; }
  try { itemFiles.push({ it, src, ...pngSize(src) }); } catch { missing.push(`Item_${it.id}(IHDR)`); }
}
itemFiles.sort((a, b) => b.h - a.h);
let atlasPage = null, atlasX = 0, atlasY = 0, shelfH = 0, atlasIdx = -1;
const flushAtlas = () => {
  if (!atlasPage) return;
  writeFileSync(join(OUT_DIR, `Item_Atlas_${atlasIdx}.png`), PNG.sync.write(atlasPage));
  atlasPage = null;
};
const newAtlasPage = () => {
  flushAtlas();
  atlasIdx++;
  atlasPage = new PNG({ width: ATLAS_W, height: ATLAS_H });
  atlasX = GUTTER; atlasY = GUTTER; shelfH = 0;
  console.log(`item atlas #${atlasIdx} open`);
};
for (const { it, src, w: iw0, h: ih0 } of itemFiles) {
  const id = it.id;
  let png = null;
  try { png = PNG.sync.read(readFileSync(src)); } catch { png = null; }
  const base = {
    name: it.name,
    key: it.key,
    createTile: it.createTile ?? null,
  };
  if (!png) {
    // 解码失败兜底:保持旧单体路径(同时拷贝单体)
    copyFileSync(src, join(OUT_DIR, `Item_${id}.png`)); copied++;
    out.items[id] = { ...base, icon: `vanilla/Item_${id}.png` };
    continue;
```

```json
 "new_string": "    // 液体交互音（WorldGen.PlayLiquidChangeSound :4577-4599，六种配对）：\n    // LavaWater{水,岩浆}/HoneyWater{水,蜜}/HoneyLava{岩浆,蜜} = liquids_* 专属轨\n    // （SoundID.cs:823-827 CreateTrackable 各 3 变体，素材已从 Sounds/Custom/ 落位\n    // public/sounds/——登记销核）；Shimmer×3{微光,水/岩浆/蜜} 原版即 PlaySound(19,…,4)\n    // = Splash_4（LegacySoundPlayer.cs:885 音量 ×0.75）。\n    // 本仓库液体编码 1水/2岩浆/3蜂蜜/4微光，配对任意序\n    this.liquid.liquidChangeSound = (x, y, a, b) => {\n      const lo = Math.min(a, b), hi = Math.max(a, b);\n      if (lo < 1 || hi > 4 || lo === hi) return; // 同类/越界不发声\n      const px = x * TILE + 8, py = y * TILE + 8;   // :4582 x*16+count*8（count 取 1）\n      if (hi === 4) { this.sfx.play('shimmerSplash', 0.75, px, py); return; }\n      const name = lo === 1 && hi === 2 ? 'liquidWaterLava'\n        : lo === 1 && hi === 3 ? 'liquidHoneyWater' : 'liquidHoneyLava';\n      this.sfx.play(name, 1, px, py);\n    };"
}
```

```json
 "new_string": "  /** 上一 tick 位置（段跟随用：段复制前一段的旧位置 = 经典贪吃蛇链） */\n  prevX = 0; prevY = 0;\n\n  // ===================== 原版 npc.oldPos/oldRot 历史缓冲（DrawNPC 拖尾族） =====================\n  // 原版 NPC.oldPos/oldRot 容量 10，UpdateNPC 头部整体后移一格 → oldPos[i] = i tick 前\n  // 的左上角坐标。Main.cs DrawNPC 拖尾族读 0..9：蠕虫族 125-131（:24660 间隔 2 取 5 份）、\n  // 火月怪 419（:24638 取 0..6 七份渐隐 + 帧行 5-7 循环）。\n  // 定长预分配（性能审计\"每帧分配热点\"清单），零逐 tick 分配；联机傀儡路径同样推入\n  // （fixedUpdate 头部早退分支内单独调用）——傀儡只有快照推进但拖尾同样需要历史。\n  private static readonly HIST_N = 10;\n  private histX = new Float64Array(Enemy.HIST_N);\n  private histY = new Float64Array(Enemy.HIST_N);\n  private histRot = new Float64Array(Enemy.HIST_N);\n  private histHead = 0;   // 最新条目下标\n  private histCount = 0;  // 已填条数（<10 时拖尾按实际深度截断）\n\n  /** fixedUpdate 尾部推入当前快照（位置=左上角，旋转=visAngle≈npc.rotation） */\n  private pushHistory(): void {\n    this.histHead = (this.histHead + 1) % Enemy.HIST_N;\n    if (this.histCount < Enemy.HIST_N) this.histCount++;\n    this.histX[this.histHead] = this.x;\n    this.histY[this.histHead] = this.y;\n    this.histRot[this.histHead] = this.visAngle;\n  }\n\n  /** i tick 前的历史条数（拖尾最大可用深度） */\n  historyDepth(): number { return this.histCount; }\n  /** i tick 前的左上角 X（i 超出已填深度时取最旧条目） */\n  histXAt(i: number): number {\n    const d = Math.min(Math.max(0, i), this.histCount - 1);\n    return this.histX[(this.histHead - d + Enemy.HIST_N * 2) % Enemy.HIST_N];\n  }\n  histYAt(i: number): number {\n    const d = Math.min(Math.max(0, i), this.histCount - 1);\n    return this.histY[(this.histHead - d + Enemy.HIST_N * 2) % Enemy.HIST_N];\n  }\n  /** i tick 前的自转角（原版 oldRot[i]） */\n  histRotAt(i: number): number {\n    const d = Math.min(Math.max(0, i), this.histCount - 1);\n    return this.histRot[(this.histHead - d + Enemy.HIST_N * 2) % Enemy.HIST_N];\n  }"
}
```

````
```ts

  if (!this.atlas) return;

  if (!g) return;

```
Hmm vmisc returns null if not loaded; ensureVImage triggers load and returns null first time. So: `const img = this.atlas.ensureVImage(g.tex); if (!img) return;` then gr = {img, sx:0, sy:0, sw:img.width, sh:img.height}. That's simpler than vmisc. But existing code uses vmisc + expects preloaded. I'll switch to ensureVImage for lazy behavior (the assets aren't in any preload list).

For 'frame' mode currently computes gh from gr.sh / frames and picks row idx from r.sy/r.sh. Keep.

Rewrite plan for drawNpcGlow:

```ts
private drawNpcGlow(
  e: Enemy,
  r: { img: CanvasImageSource; sx: number; sy: number; sw: number; sh: number },
  bx: number, by: number,   // 基础精灵在本变换内的目标左上（与本体同锚对齐）
) {
  if (!this.atlas) return;
  const g = Renderer.NPC_GLOW[e.vanillaId ?? -1];
  if (!g) return;
  const gimg = this.atlas.ensureVImage(g.tex);   // 懒加载（首帧 null，次帧生效）
  if (!gimg) return;
  const ctx = this.ctx;
  const frames = Math.max(1, e.vanilla?.frames ?? 1);
  const scale = (e.vanilla?.scale ?? 1) * e.vanillaScale;
  const opacity = 1 - Math.min(255, Math.max(e.spawnAlpha, 0)) / 255;
  const gh = Math.max(1, Math.floor(gimg.height / frames));
  const row = Math.min(frames - 1, Math.floor(r.sy / Math.max(1, r.sh)));
  const gAlpha = (127 - Math.min(255, Math.max(e.spawnAlpha,0)) / 2) / 255; // 127-α/2

  ctx.save();
  ctx.globalCompositeOperation = 'lighter';
  if (g.mode === 'frame') {
    // ... existing alpha branches for 551/564/565/548/通用
    ctx.globalAlpha = ...;
    ctx.drawImage(gimg, 0, Math.min(gimg.height - gh, row * gh), gimg.width, gh, bx, by, r.sw, r.sh);
    if (g.lamp) { ... Extra_20 + glow ... }
  } else if (g.mode === 'vel4') {
    const num = g.velMult ?? 4;
    ctx.globalAlpha = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, (64 / 255) * opacity));
    for (let k = 0; k < 4; k++) {
      const a = k * Math.PI / 2;
      const dx = ((e.vx * Math.cos(a) - e.vy * Math.sin(a)) * num) / scale;
````

```json
 "old_string": "  //   mode 'frame'   同帧整图叠加（火星飞碟族/Betsy/暗黑魔法师/永恒水晶/银河织带）\n  //   mode 'vel4'    以速度向量四向旋转偏移画 4 份灰 A=64 拷贝（四塔 + 火星飞骑 416）\n  // 未接（登记）：Glow_100/101（case 94 前）、Glow_154（case 384 拖尾）、Glow_146\n  // （case 662 族）、Glow_156（火星飞骑 416 :24531-24535，同 vel4 模式但锚点是\n  // 底心 +4px 特例）——前三处依赖 oldPos 拖尾链，本渲染层无逐 NPC 历史缓冲；\n  // Projectile glowMask（:34327-34378）与 Item glowMask（:38072-38213）不在本批。\n  private static readonly NPC_GLOW: Record<number, { tex: string; mode: 'frame' | 'vel4' }> = {\n    392: { tex: 'vanilla/Glow_48.png', mode: 'frame' },   // 火星飞碟主体（:24218 Color(200,200,200,0)）\n    395: { tex: 'vanilla/Glow_49.png', mode: 'frame' },   // 飞碟核心（:24222）\n    394: { tex: 'vanilla/Glow_50.png', mode: 'frame' },   // 飞碟炮塔（:24226）\n    551: { tex: 'vanilla/Glow_226.png', mode: 'frame' },  // Betsy（:23099 Lerp(color,White,0.6) A=66）\n    564: { tex: 'vanilla/Glow_225.png', mode: 'frame' },  // 暗黑魔法师 T1（:23480 white×Opacity A/2）\n    565: { tex: 'vanilla/Glow_225.png', mode: 'frame' },  // 暗黑魔法师 T2\n    548: { tex: 'vanilla/Glow_239.png', mode: 'frame' },  // 永恒水晶（:23594 呼吸脉冲底层）\n    402: { tex: 'vanilla/Glow_135.png', mode: 'frame' },  // 银河织带头（:23886 白 A=255-num291）\n    422: { tex: 'vanilla/Glow_149.png', mode: 'vel4' },   // 太阳塔（:23766）\n    493: { tex: 'vanilla/Glow_132.png', mode: 'vel4' },   // 星尘塔（:23746）\n    507: { tex: 'vanilla/Glow_143.png', mode: 'vel4' },   // 星云塔（:23756）\n    517: { tex: 'vanilla/Glow_162.png', mode: 'vel4' },   // 星璇塔（:23776，+UnitX*2 偏移）\n  };\n\n  /** GlowMask 叠画：与基础精灵同一变换（调用点在 drawEnemy 的 save 块内，\n   *  原点=精灵锚点），按表内 mode 分流。additive（XNA 默认 AlphaBlend + 贴图\n   *  自带发光色的语义，用 lighter 还原\"叠加提亮\"观感） */\n  private drawNpcGlow(e: Enemy, r: { img: CanvasImageSource; sx: number; sy: number; sw: number; sh: number }) {\n    if (!this.atlas) return;\n    const g = Renderer.NPC_GLOW[e.vanillaId ?? -1];\n    if (!g) return;\n    const gr = this.atlas.vmisc(g.tex);\n    if (!gr) return;\n    const ctx = this.ctx;\n    const scale = (e.vanilla?.scale ?? 1) * e.vanillaScale;\n    const opacity = 1 - Math.min(255, Math.max(e.spawnAlpha, 0)) / 255; // NPC.Opacity\n    ctx.save();\n    ctx.globalCompositeOperation = 'lighter';\n    if (g.mode === 'frame') {\n      // 同帧整图叠加：源矩形取 Glow_N 的对应帧带（Glow 表帧高 = 表高/帧数；\n      // 与 NPC 表帧高的逐像素对齐未逐一校验——登记，观感为发光罩层）\n      const gh = Math.max(1, Math.floor(gr.sh / Math.max(1, e.vanilla?.frames ?? 1)));\n      const idx = Math.min((e.vanilla?.frames ?? 1) - 1, Math.floor(r.sy / Math.max(1, r.sh)));\n      let alpha = 0.8;\n      let flashPulse = 1;\n      if (e.vanillaId === 551) { alpha = (66 / 255) * 1.3; }              // :23099 A=66 ×(0.7+0.3*lerp)\n      else if (e.vanillaId === 564 || e.vanillaId === 565) { alpha = 0.5 * opacity; } // :23484 white.A/2×Opacity\n      else if (e.vanillaId === 548) {\n        // :23590-23596 num63/65 三秒呼吸三角波 ×0.6 紫底（(140,50,255)）\n        const t3 = (performance.now() / 1000) % 3 / 3;\n        flashPulse = t3 > 0.5 ? 1 - t3 : t3;\n        alpha = 0.6 * Math.max(0, flashPulse);\n      } else alpha = 0.78;                                                  // Color(200,200,200,0)/白色系通用\n      ctx.globalAlpha = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, alpha));\n      ctx.drawImage(gr.img, gr.sx, Math.min(gr.sh - gh, idx * gh), gr.sw, gh,\n        -r.sw / 2, -r.sh / 2, r.sw, r.sh);\n    } else {\n      // vel4：velocity.RotatedBy(k*π/2) × num 偏移画 4 份（:23750/:23850 Color(64,64,64,0)）\n      // ——原版在世界坐标偏移，此处转回本变换局部坐标（当前原点=精灵锚点，需平移 (cx,cy)-锚）\n      const num = 4 + 0 * 4; // (GetAlpha-0.5).Length()*4 光照项恒 0（我们无逐 NPC 光照）\n      ctx.globalAlpha = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, (64 / 255) * opacity));\n      for (let k = 0; k < 4; k++) {\n        const a = k * Math.PI / 2;\n        // 世界坐标偏移 → 本变换局部（当前原点=精灵锚点，除 scale 还原）\n        const dx = ((e.vx * Math.cos(a) - e.vy * Math.sin(a)) * num) / scale;\n        const dy = ((e.vx * Math.sin(a) + e.vy * Math.cos(a)) * num) / scale\n          + (e.vanillaId === 517 ? 2 : 0);\n        ctx.drawImage(gr.img, gr.sx, gr.sy, gr.sw, gr.sh, -r.sw / 2 + dx, -r.sh / 2 + dy, r.sw, r.sh);\n      }\n    }\n    ctx.restore();\n  }",
 "new_string": "  //   mode 'frame'   同帧整图叠加（火星飞碟族/Betsy/暗黑魔法师/永恒水晶/银河织带/探测器/吮脑怪）\n  //   mode 'vel4'    以速度向量四向旋转偏移画 4 份灰 A=64 拷贝（四塔 + 火星飞骑 416）\n  //   mode 'trail'   419 火月怪：本体罩 + vel4/UnitX4 + ai2<=-9 时 oldPos 0..6 拖尾\n  //                  （:24610-24645，Enemy.historyDepth/histXAt 历史缓冲）\n  // 第二批已接（原\"未接登记\"销核）：Glow_100/101+Extra_20（399 探测器 :24564-24575）、\n  // Glow_156（416 火龙怪骑士 :24531-24535 底心 +4px 锚 + vel4×0.5）、Glow_154（419 :25677-25696\n  // + 拖尾）、Glow_146（421 吮脑怪 :25618-25621 A=128-α/2）。\n  // Projectile glowMask（:34327-34378）与 Item glowMask（:38072-38213）不在本批。\n  private static readonly NPC_GLOW: Record<number, {\n    tex: string; mode: 'frame' | 'vel4' | 'trail';\n    /** vel4 速度偏移倍率（四塔 4；416 骑手 :24538 为 0.5 + 光照项） */\n    velMult?: number;\n    /** 399 探测器状态灯（:24571-24575）：Extra_20(ai0+1 行) 本体 + glow 罩，dy=+24px */\n    lamp?: { tex: string; glow: string; dy: number };\n  }> = {\n    392: { tex: 'vanilla/Glow_48.png', mode: 'frame' },   // 火星飞碟主体（:24218 Color(200,200,200,0)）\n    395: { tex: 'vanilla/Glow_49.png', mode: 'frame' },   // 飞碟核心（:24222）\n    394: { tex: 'vanilla/Glow_50.png', mode: 'frame' },   // 飞碟炮塔（:24226）\n    551: { tex: 'vanilla/Glow_226.png', mode: 'frame' },  // Betsy（:23099 Lerp(color,White,0.6) A=66）\n    564: { tex: 'vanilla/Glow_225.png', mode: 'frame' },  // 暗黑魔法师 T1（:23480 white×Opacity A/2）\n    565: { tex: 'vanilla/Glow_225.png', mode: 'frame' },  // 暗黑魔法师 T2\n    548: { tex: 'vanilla/Glow_239.png', mode: 'frame' },  // 永恒水晶（:23594 呼吸脉冲底层）\n    402: { tex: 'vanilla/Glow_135.png', mode: 'frame' },  // 银河织带头（:23886 白 A=255-num291）\n    422: { tex: 'vanilla/Glow_149.png', mode: 'vel4' },   // 太阳塔（:23766）\n    493: { tex: 'vanilla/Glow_132.png', mode: 'vel4' },   // 星尘塔（:23746）\n    507: { tex: 'vanilla/Glow_143.png', mode: 'vel4' },   // 星云塔（:23756）\n    517: { tex: 'vanilla/Glow_162.png', mode: 'vel4' },   // 星璇塔（:23776，+UnitX*2 偏移）\n    // ---- 第二批（底锚/拖尾族）----\n    399: { tex: 'vanilla/Glow_100.png', mode: 'frame',   // 火星探测器：本体罩 A=127-α/2（:24564）\n      lamp: { tex: 'vanilla/Extra_20.png', glow: 'vanilla/Glow_101.png', dy: 24 } },\n    416: { tex: 'vanilla/Glow_156.png', mode: 'vel4', velMult: 0.5 },  // 火龙怪骑士（:24531-24535）\n    419: { tex: 'vanilla/Glow_154.png', mode: 'trail' }, // 火月怪（:25677-25696 + :24610-24645 拖尾）\n    421: { tex: 'vanilla/Glow_146.png', mode: 'frame' }, // 吮脑怪：底锚 A=128-α/2（:25618-25621）\n  };\n\n  /** GlowMask 叠画：与基础精灵同一变换（调用点在 drawEnemy 的 save 块内）。\n   *  bx/by = 基础精灵在本变换内的目标左上（与本体严格同锚——地面型底锚/飞行型\n   *  中心锚由调用方传入，各层不再自行假设锚点）。\n   *  additive（XNA 默认 AlphaBlend + 贴图自带发光色的语义，用 lighter 还原\n   *  \"叠加提亮\"观感） */\n  private drawNpcGlow(\n    e: Enemy,\n    r: { img: CanvasImageSource; sx: number; sy: number; sw: number; sh: number },\n    bx: number, by: number,\n  ) {\n    if (!this.atlas) return;\n    const g = Renderer.NPC_GLOW[e.vanillaId ?? -1];\n    if (!g) return;\n    const img = this.atlas.ensureVImage(g.tex);   // 懒加载（首帧 null，次帧生效）\n    if (!img) return;\n    const ctx = this.ctx;\n    const frames = Math.max(1, e.vanilla?.frames ?? 1);\n    const scale = (e.vanilla?.scale ?? 1) * e.vanillaScale;\n    const alphaRaw = Math.min(255, Math.max(e.spawnAlpha, 0));\n    const opacity = 1 - alphaRaw / 255; // NPC.Opacity\n    // Glow 表与 NPC 表同布局（同宽 × 帧数行）；帧行 = 当前帧（原版一律 rCurrentNPC.frame）\n    const gh = Math.max(1, Math.floor(img.height / frames));\n    const row = Math.min(frames - 1, Math.max(0, Math.floor(r.sy / Math.max(1, r.sh))));\n    const sy = Math.min(img.height - gh, row * gh);\n    ctx.save();\n    ctx.globalCompositeOperation = 'lighter';\n    if (g.mode === 'frame') {\n      let alpha = 0.8;\n      let flashPulse = 1;\n      if (e.vanillaId === 551) { alpha = (66 / 255) * 1.3; }              // :23099 A=66 ×(0.7+0.3*lerp)\n      else if (e.vanillaId === 564 || e.vanillaId === 565) { alpha = 0.5 * opacity; } // :23484 white.A/2×Opacity\n      else if (e.vanillaId === 548) {\n        // :23590-23596 num63/65 三秒呼吸三角波 ×0.6 紫底（(140,50,255)）\n        const t3 = (performance.now() / 1000) % 3 / 3;\n        flashPulse = t3 > 0.5 ? 1 - t3 : t3;\n        alpha = 0.6 * Math.max(0, flashPulse);\n      } else if (e.vanillaId === 399) { alpha = (127 - alphaRaw / 2) / 255; }  // :24568 Color(127-α/2,…)\n      else if (e.vanillaId === 421) { alpha = (128 - alphaRaw / 2) / 255; }    // :25622 Color(128-α/2,…)\n      else alpha = 0.78;                                                  // Color(200,200,200,0)/白色系通用\n      ctx.globalAlpha = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, alpha));\n      ctx.drawImage(img, 0, sy, img.width, gh, bx, by, r.sw, r.sh);\n      // 399 探测器状态灯（:24571-24575）：Extra_20 按 ai0+1 取行，画在本体下方 +24px，\n      // 再叠 Glow_101 同源矩形罩（A=127-α/2）\n      if (g.lamp) {\n        const lamp = this.atlas.ensureVImage(g.lamp.tex);\n        const lampGlow = this.atlas.ensureVImage(g.lamp.glow);\n        if (lamp && lampGlow) {\n          const rows = 4, lh = lamp.height / rows;\n          const lrow = Math.max(0, Math.min(rows - 1, Math.floor(e.ai0) + 1)); // Frame(1,4,0,ai0+1)\n          const lx = -lamp.width / 2;                    // 水平居中（原版 X=本体 drawPos.X=中心）\n          const ly = by + g.lamp.dy / scale;             // 世界 px +24 → 局部除 scale\n          ctx.globalAlpha = opacity;                     // GetAlpha(npcColor)\n          ctx.drawImage(lamp, 0, lrow * lh, lamp.width, lh, lx, ly, lamp.width, lh);\n          ctx.globalAlpha = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, (127 - alphaRaw / 2) / 255));\n          ctx.drawImage(lampGlow, 0, lrow * lh, lampGlow.width, lh, lx, ly, lamp.width, lh);\n        }\n      }\n    } else if (g.mode === 'vel4') {\n      // vel4：velocity.RotatedBy(k*π/2) × num 偏移画 4 份灰 A=64（:23750/:24535）\n      const num = g.velMult ?? 4; // (GetAlpha-0.5).Length()*k 光照项恒 0（我们无逐 NPC 光照）\n      ctx.globalAlpha = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, (64 / 255) * opacity));\n      for (let k = 0; k < 4; k++) {\n        const a = k * Math.PI / 2;\n        // 世界坐标偏移 → 本变换局部（当前原点=精灵锚点，除 scale 还原）\n        const dx = ((e.vx * Math.cos(a) - e.vy * Math.sin(a)) * num) / scale;\n        const dy = ((e.vx * Math.sin(a) + e.vy * Math.cos(a)) * num) / scale\n          + (e.vanillaId === 517 ? 2 : 0);\n        ctx.drawImage(img, 0, sy, img.width, gh, bx + dx, by + dy, r.sw, r.sh);\n      }\n    } else {\n      // trail：419 火月怪（:25677-25696 本体罩 + 分支四向残影；:24610-24645 拖尾）\n      // 本体罩 Color(255-α,…) → 全亮白×Opacity\n      ctx.globalAlpha = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, opacity));\n      ctx.drawImage(img, 0, sy, img.width, gh, bx, by, r.sw, r.sh);\n      ctx.globalAlpha = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, (64 / 255) * opacity));\n      if (e.ai2 >= -6) {\n        // vel4 ×0.5（:25681-25686 num208 = 0.5 + 光照项）\n        for (let k = 0; k < 4; k++) {\n          const a = k * Math.PI / 2;\n          const dx = ((e.vx * Math.cos(a) - e.vy * Math.sin(a)) * 0.5) / scale;\n          const dy = ((e.vx * Math.sin(a) + e.vy * Math.cos(a)) * 0.5) / scale;\n          ctx.drawImage(img, 0, sy, img.width, gh, bx + dx, by + dy, r.sw, r.sh);\n        }\n      } else {\n        // UnitX.RotatedBy(k*π/2)×4：固定四向 4px（:25688-25697 num210=4）\n        for (let k = 0; k < 4; k++) {\n          const dx = (Math.cos(k * Math.PI / 2) * 4) / scale;\n          const dy = (Math.sin(k * Math.PI / 2) * 4) / scale;\n          ctx.drawImage(img, 0, sy, img.width, gh, bx + dx, by + dy, r.sw, r.sh);\n        }\n      }\n      // 旋刃冲刺拖尾（:24610-24645，ai2<=-9）：oldPos 0..6 七份，帧行 (curRow-3-i)%3+5\n      // 循环、scale Lerp(0.75,1.2,(10-i)/10)、白度 (10-i)/20 渐隐。独立平移到历史\n      // 位置（世界坐标）——bx/by 恰为\"底锚相对偏移\"，直接复用\n      if (e.ai2 <= -9) {\n        const depth = Math.min(7, e.historyDepth());\n        for (let i = Math.min(6, depth - 1); i >= 0; i--) {\n          let trow = (row - 3 - i) % 3;\n          if (trow < 0) trow += 3;\n          trow += 5;\n          const tsy = Math.min(img.height - gh, trow * gh);\n          const ts = 0.75 + 0.45 * ((10 - i) / 10);   // Lerp(0.75,1.2,(10-i)/10)\n          ctx.save();\n          ctx.translate(e.histXAt(i) + e.w / 2, e.histYAt(i) + e.h);\n          ctx.rotate(e.histRotAt(i));\n          ctx.scale(ts, ts);\n          ctx.globalAlpha = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, (10 - i) / 20)); // White×(10-i)/20, A=0\n          ctx.drawImage(img, 0, tsy, img.width, gh, bx, by, r.sw, r.sh);\n          ctx.restore();\n        }\n      }\n    }\n    ctx.restore();\n  }"
}
```

```
- `Renderer`：新 mode `'trail'`；入表 399(Glow_100+Extra_20/Glow_101 状态灯 ai0+1 行)、416(Glow_156 vel4×0.5)、419(Glow_154)、421(Glow_146 A=128-α/2)。419 拖尾 1:1：ai2≤-9 → oldPos 0..6 七份、帧行 (curRow-3-i)%3+5、scale Lerp(0.75,1.2)、alpha (10-i)/20 additive
- drawNpcGlow 改传基础精灵目标矩形(bx/by)——发光层与本体同锚（顺带修复地面型发光层错位 latent bug）；vel4 改切当前帧行（原版恒用 rCurrentNPC.frame）
- **素材核对**：Glow_100/101/146/154/156 + Extra_20 全在 terraria-assets/Images，已拷入 public/sprites/vanilla 并入 vanilla-atlas.mjs MISC 白名单
- **纠偏登记**：Glow_100/101/146 原版并非拖尾（静态帧罩），仅 Glow_154(419) 是——原登记归类有误，已按 Main.cs 实况实现
```
